# burn-down in TODO §15; it is NOT a place to add new exceptions (use
# house_style_allow.txt for a reviewed false positive) and it never carries a
# `clippy-allow` entry.

# One JSON object per line:
#   {"path": ..., "rule": ..., "anchor": ..., "reason": ...}
#     path    repo-relative file.
#     rule    the finding rule (`reference-citation` or `history-comment`).
#     anchor  the normalized (whitespace-collapsed) offending comment line.
#             Matching is `anchor in normalized(source line)` keyed by path,
#             rule and anchor, so a line shift does not make it stale; a
#             rewritten comment does (reported as a stale baseline note).
#     reason  "legacy comment, cleanup tracked in TODO §15".
